主席報告書
CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT

                                                       2018年 ，國內經濟呈現下行趨勢 ，經濟增速減緩 ，且因
                                                       中美貿易摩擦的升級 ，對外貿易情況受到一定影響 。在國
                                                       內經濟不確定性增強的情況下 ，氟硅行業也在本年度出現
                                                       了一定波動 ，下游需求變動導致部分產品價格有所下滑 。
                                                       然而 ，在波動的大環境下 ，東岳仍然憑借自身的各方面優
                                                       勢 ，於年度內創造了良好的業績 ，保持了較高幅度的業績
                                                       增長 ，取得了令人滿意的成績 。

                                                       In 2018, the domestic economy showed a downward trend
                                                       in economic growth, and the foreign trade was impaired
                                                       to a certain extent due to the escalation of trade conflicts
                                                       between China and the United States. During the year, the
                                                       fluorosilicone industry experienced certain fluctuations in
                                                       the face of increased domestic economic uncertainties, and
                                                       the changes in the downstream demands resulted in the
                                                       decline of some products’ prices. However, in the turbulent
                                                       environment, Dongyue still maintained relatively high
                                                       performance, and achieved satisfactory results during the
                                                       year by virtue of its various advantages.




                                                       ZHANG JIANHONG Chairman
                                                       張建宏 主席

                                   production

     念 ，使本集團近幾年在行業變化中立於不敗          standards of safety and environmental protection have enabled
     之地 。年內 ，本集團進一步形成集團安全環         itself to weather all the changes in the industry in recent years

     園區內建成淨水廠 ，實現了園區內水的循環          concept introduced has initially achieved results in the Group,
     綜合利用 ，減少了水的外排量 。              effectively reducing the safety risks of its subsidiaries. The
                                   Group has built a water purification plant in the park of the

                                   amount of water discharged.

     大做強東岳硅材的業務 ，提升其競爭力和行            Organosilicone would expand its financing channels, enhance
     業地位 ，加大科技 、環保的投入 ，提高盈           its development potential and strengthen its own business as

根據蒙特利爾議定書 ，R22將於2030年末前被逐                  Pursuant to the Montreal Protocol, R22 would be progressively phased
步淘汰 ，由其他綠色製冷劑取代 。中國政府已                     out as a refrigerant by the end of 2030 and would be replaced by other
不再批准任何作為製冷劑使用的新增R22產能 。                    green refrigerants. The Chinese government has stopped granting
由2013年開始 ，中國政府已對國內所有R22生產                  approval to the building up of new capacity for R22. Starting from 2013,
商就其銷售R22作為製冷劑實施配額制 。另一方                    the Chinese government has been enforcing a quota system for the
面 ，雖然本集團擁有大量的R22產能 ，但由於其                   sales of R22 as a refrigerant for all of the domestic R22 producers. On
可以用作生產含氟高分子材料的原材料使用 ，本                     the other hand, R22 can be used as raw material for the production
集團調整削減的R22用於生產含氟高分子材料 ，                    of the Fluoropolymer. Therefore, although the Group has a huge
以滿足日益增長的含氟高分子材料市場的需求 。                     production capacity of R22, the saved production capacity of R22 as
                                           a refrigerant was transformed and utilized by the Group to produce
                                           Fluoropolymer in order to cope with the increasing market demand for

     金融服務主協議將為本集團提供另一全新渠道 ，以                 The FS Agreement will provide the Group with a new means to utilize its
     較低資本風險善用剩餘資金 ，並以較低融資成本取                 surplus cash at a low capital risk and obtain financings at a lower financing

肩負為本公司股東（「股東」）創造價值及帶來最大      “Company”) recognizes their mission of creating values and maximizing
回報的使命 ，同時履行其企業責任 。為此 ，本公     returns to the shareholders of the Company (the “Shareholders”), while
司矢志提升及保持最高水準的企業管治 。          at the same time fulfilling their corporate responsibilities. To this end,
                             we strive to promote and uphold the highest standard of corporate
                             governance.

主席與行政總裁的角色                     Roles of chairman and chief executive officer
守則第A.2.1條規定 ，主席與行政總裁的角色應有      Code A.2.1 of the Code stipulates that the roles of chairman and chief
區分 ，且不應由同一人兼任 。張建宏先生目前身        executive officer should be separate and should not be performed
兼本公司主席與行政總裁 。董事會認為 ，由一人        by the same individual. Mr. Zhang Jianhong is both the Chairman
同時兼任主席及行政總裁可令本集團更能貫徹強          and Chief Executive Officer of the Company. The Board believes that
勢領導 ，且在業務策劃上更具效率 。此外 ，董        vesting the roles of both Chairman and Chief Executive Officer in the
事會認為 ，在由多名經驗豐富且具才幹的人士所         same position provides the Group with stronger and more consistent
組成董事會密切監控下 ，此架構不會影響權力平         leadership and allows for more effective planning. Further, the Board
衡 。董事會對張先生充滿信心 ，並深信現時安排        considers that this structure will not impair the balance of power, which
對本集團的業務前景最為有利 。                has been closely monitored by the Board, which comprises experienced
                               and high caliber individuals. The Board has full confidence in Mr. Zhang
                               and believes that the current arrangement is beneficial to the business
                               prospect of the Group.

的專業知識 ，以為本公司作出貢獻 ，且有足夠時        Company. The Board considers that the new Director(s) is expected to

此外 ，有關董事會成員多元化政策的概要載列如         In addition, a summary of the Policy for the diversity of the Board is set
下：                             out as follows:


A.   政策聲明                      A.   Policy Statement
     本公司明白並深信建立一個多元的董事會的            The Company recognizes and embraces the benefits of having a
     裨益良多 。一個真正多元的董事會可容入並           diverse Board. A truly diverse Board will include and make good
     善用董事的不同技能 、地區及行業經驗 、背          use of differences in the skills, regional and industry experience,
     景 、種族 、性別及其他資歷 。此等差異將          background, race, gender and other qualities of Directors. These

                          公司董事會參與社會責任重大事項的審議與決策，包括相

     4.2 誠信合規                             4.2 Integrity and Compliance
       誠信合規是公司管治的重點 ，也是公                      Integrity and compliance are key issues with respect to
       司長遠發展的基石 。公司嚴格遵守《關                     corporate governance, and serve as the cornerstone for the
       於辦理商業賄賂刑事案件適用法律若干                      Company’s sustainable development. In strict compliance
       問題的意見》《關於辦理貪污賄賂刑事案                     with the Opinions on Issues concerning the Application
       件適用法律若干問題的解釋》等相關法                      of Law in the Handling of Criminal Cases of Commercial
       律法規 ，不斷健全反腐敗管治機制 ，                     Briberies, Interpretation on Several Issues concerning the
       持續加強制度建設 。公司制定《東岳集                     Application of Law in the Handling of Criminal Cases of
       團風險管理手冊》，以健金風險管理體                      Embezzlement and Bribery and other laws and regulations,
       系 ，識別和管理公司風險；制定《東岳                     the Company continued to improve its governance
       集團反舞弊行為管理規定》，以規範員                      mechanism against corruption and strengthened its
       工職業行為 ，防治舞弊 。                          institutional improvement on an ongoing basis. The
                                              Company developed the Dongyue Group Risk Management
                                              Manual to improve its risk management system, and to
                                              identify and control the Company’s risk. The Company also
                                              developed the Dongyue Group Regulations on Anti-fraud
                                              Management to regulate employee professional behavior
                                              and prevent fraud.


       2018年 ，公司下發《關於集團黨員幹部                   In 2018, the Company issued the Notice on Integrity and
       廉潔安全過春節的通知》，要求集團全                      Safety for Party Members and Cadres of the Group during
       體幹部員工嚴格執行節日八項禁令 ，                      the Chinese New Year which stipulated that eight rules must
       並明確要求特崗部門加強節日自律和業                      be strictly followed by all cadres of the Group and required
       務單位管理 。同時 ，公司與協作單位                     special task department to strengthen discipline and business
       簽訂《商業廉潔合作關係責任書》，與黨                     unit management during the holiday. Meanwhile, the
       員幹部 、特崗人員簽訂《黨員幹部 、特                    Company entered into the Letter of Responsibility regarding
       崗人員廉潔自律責任書》，提升協作單                      Honest Business Cooperation with cooperative units, and
       位及員工的廉潔意識 。此外 ，公司還                     entered into the Letter of Responsibility regarding Integrity
       下發《東岳集團黨員幹部操辦婚喪喜慶                      and Discipline of Party Members and Cadres and Members
       事宜的規定》，要求全體幹部要廉潔自                      in the Special Tasks Department with party members and
       律 ，並對操辦婚喪喜慶實行報告制度 。                    cadres and members in the special tasks department in an
                                              attempt to raise the integrity awareness of the cooperative
                                              units and its employees. In addition, the Company also issued
                                              Rules regarding Weddings and Funerals of Party Members
                                              and Cadres of Dongyue Group which required all cadres to
                                              be commit themselves to integrity and discipline and report

  公司設立舉報信箱和舉報電話 ，鼓勵         The Company established reporting boxes and reporting

6 安全保障                                 6 Security
     秉承「安全是東岳的第一價值」的理念 ，本集                Under the guidelines of “Safety is Dongyue’s No.1 Value”, the
     團嚴格遵守《中華人民共和國安全生產法》等                 Group strictly abides by the national laws and regulations such as
     國家法律法規 ，執行保障安全生產的相關國                 the “Safe Production Law of the People’s Republic of China” and
     家標準 。公司從風險管控機制和安全文化機                 carefully implements the national standards in relation to safe
     制著手 ，致力於建立和固化內部HSE體系標                production. The Company started from its risk control mechanism
     準 。2018年集團新增《東岳集團十大高危行               and safety culture, and strived to establish and consolidate
     為考核管理辦法》   《不可違背安全條例》等制              its internal HSE standards. In 2018, the Group formulated new

案例：東岳張家界三台山村扶貧項目        Case: Poverty alleviation project of Santaishan Village,
                        Zhangjiajie of Dongyue


三台山村是湖南省省級貧困村 ，東岳       Santaishan Village is a provincial-level poor village in Hunan.
集團積極響應精準扶貧工作 ，助推扶       Dongyue Group responded to poverty alleviation initiatives
貧事業 ，向永定區慈善總會捐贈200萬     in a proactive manner and facilitated its poverty alleviation
元 ，用於三台山東岳養老院建設 。       business through donation of RMB2 million to Yongding
                        District Charity Association for the construction of Santaishan
                        Dongyue nursing home.


2018年三台山東岳養老院正式竣工 ，共    In 2018, the construction of Santaishan Dongyue nursing
12間房 ，可同時容納24人居住 。養老    home was officially completed. The nursing home has a
院環境優美 、設施齊全 ，已成為張家      total of 12 rooms that can accommodate 24 people at the
界市社會主義精神文明建設的標誌性建       same time. It is well-equipped, surrounded with tranquil
築 ，實現了三台山老人「老有所居 、老     environment and has become a landmark for socialist
有所養 、老有所依 ，老有所樂」，促進     spiritual civilization construction in Zhangjiajie to realize
當地旅遊資源的開發利用 ，助力村民       that all elderly people in Santaishan are provided with
脫貧致富 。                  residence, living support, love and recreational facilities,
                        thereby promoting the development and utilization of the
                        local tourism resources and facilitating poverty alleviation for
                        villagers.

 東 岳 集 團 有 限 公 司（「 本 公 司 」）於2006年7              Dongyue Group Limited (the “Company”) was incorporated in the
 月24日 於 開 曼 群 島 註 冊 成 立 為 受 豁 免 有 限             Cayman Islands on 24 July 2006 as an exempted company with
 公司，其註冊辦事處位於Scotia Centre, 4th                  limited liability. The address of its registered office is Scotia Centre,
 Floor, P. O. Box 2804, George Town, Grand      4th Floor, P. O. Box 2804, George Town, Grand Cayman, Cayman
 Cayman, Cayman Islands。 本 公 司 的 股 份 自          Islands. The shares of the Company have been listed on the Main
 2007年12月10日起在香港聯合交易所有限公                        Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited since 10
 司主板上市。                                         December 2007.


 本公司為一間投資控股公司，其附屬公司的                            The Company is an investment holding company. The principal
 主要業務載於附註35。                                    activities of its subsidiaries are set out in note 35.


 本公司及其附屬公司（合稱「本集團」）主要從                          The Company and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to
 事製造、分銷及銷售製冷劑、高分子材料、                            as the “Group”) are principally engaged in the manufacture,
 有機硅及二氯甲烷、聚氯乙烯（「PVC」）及燒                         distribution and sale of refrigerants, polymers, organic silicone
 鹼以及其他。此外，本集團亦在中華人民共                            and dichloromethane, polyvinyl chloride (“PVC”) and liquid alkali
 和國（「中國」）從事物業開發。                                and others. In addition, the Group is also engaged in property
                                                development in the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”).


 本公司主要營業地點於年報「公司資料」一節                           The addresses of principal place of business of the Company are
 中披露。                                           disclosed in the section headed “Corporate Information” in the
                                                annual report.


 綜合財務報表以本公司及其附屬公司之功能                            The consolidated financial statements are presented in Renminbi
 貨幣人民幣（「人民幣」）呈列。                                (“RMB”), which is the same as the functional currency of the
                                                Company and its subsidiaries.

 產或負債的特點，則本集團於估計資產或負       the Group takes into account the characteristics of the asset or

 計準則第17號範圍內的租賃交易，以及與公      basis, except for share-based payment transactions that are within

 出於財務報告目的，部分資產及負債在報告       For financial reporting purposes, some assets and liabilities

 號公平值計量須就公平值計量如何分類作出       Disclosures are required by IFRS 13 Fair Value Measurement as to
 披露。根據國際財務報告準則第13號，公平      how fair value measurements are categorised. Under IFRS 13, fair

 第一層輸入值是指於計量日期由相同資產或       Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets
 負債在活躍市場中（未經調整）的報價得出；      for identical assets or liabilities that the entity can access at the
 第二層輸入值是指由除了第一層輸入值所包       measurement date; Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted

 末尚未完成，則本集團將報告未完成列賬項        by the end of the reporting period in which the combination
 目之臨時金額。該等臨時金額乃於計量期間        occurs, the Group reports provisional amounts for the items for
 內作出調整（見上文），而新增資產或負債則       which the accounting is incomplete. Those provisional amounts
 獲確認以反映關於在收購日期已存在之事實        are adjusted during the measurement period (see above), or
 及情況之新資訊，而當時如獲知該等資訊則        additional assets or liabilities are recognised, to reflect new
 可能對於該日期確認之金額造成影響。          information obtained about facts and circumstances that existed
                            as of the acquisition date that, if known, would have affected the
                            amounts recognised as of that date.

 於聯營公司的投資                 Investments in associates
 聯營公司為本集團具有重大影響力的實體。      An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant
 重大影響力指參與被投資方財務及營運政策      influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in the
 決定的權力，但並非控制或共同控制該等政      financial and operating policy decisions of the investee but is not
 策。                       control or joint control over those policies.


 聯營公司的業績以及資產及負債以權益會計      The results and assets and liabilities of associates are incorporated
 法於該等綜合財務報表入賬。作權益會計法      in these consolidated financial statements using the equity
 用途之聯營公司之財務報表乃按與本集團就      method of accounting. The financial statements of associates
 同類交易及同類情況下事項之統一會計政策      used for equity accounting purposes are prepared using uniform
 編製。根據權益法，於聯營公司的投資初步      accounting policies as those of the Group for like transactions
 按成本於綜合財務狀況表確認，並於其後就      and events in similar circumstances. Under the equity method,
 確認本集團應佔該聯營公司的損益及其他全      an investment in an associate is initially recognised in the
 面收益予以調整。當本集團應佔聯營公司的      consolidated statement of financial position at cost and adjusted
 虧損超出本集團於該聯營公司的權益（包括      thereafter to recognise the Group’s share of the profit or loss
 實質上成為本集團於該聯營公司投資淨額一      and other comprehensive income of the associate. When the
 部分的任何長期權益）時，本集團取消確認      Group’s share of losses of an associate exceeds the Group’s
 其應佔的進一步虧損。僅於本集團已產生法      interest in that associate (which includes any long-term interests
 定或推定責任，或已代表該聯營公司支付款      that, in substance, form part of the Group’s net investment in the
 項的情況下，方會確認額外虧損。          associate), the Group discontinues recognizing its share of further

                          the Group has incurred legal or constructive obligations or made

 值的任何部分確認為商譽，並計入投資的賬      the net fair value of the identifiable assets and liabilities of the

       暫時差額確認。遞延稅項資產一般在很可能                   computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally
       出現可利用暫時差額扣稅的應課稅溢利時就                   recognised for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax
       所有可扣減暫時差額確認。若於一項交易                    assets are generally recognised for all deductible temporary
       中，因商譽或因業務合併以外原因首次確認                   difference to the extent that it is probable that taxable profits will
       其他資產及負債而引致的暫時差額既不影響                   be available against which those deductible temporary differences
       應課稅溢利，亦不影響會計溢利，則不會確                   can be utilised. Such assets and liabilities are not recognised if
       認該等資產及負債。                             the temporary difference arises from goodwill or from the initial
                                             recognition (other than in a business combination) of other assets
                                             and liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the taxable profit
                                             nor the accounting profit.


       遞延稅項負債乃按因於附屬公司及聯營公司                   Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary
       的投資而引致的應課稅暫時差額而確認，惟                   differences associated with investments in subsidiaries and
       若本集團可控制暫時差額的撥回及暫時差額                   associates, except where the Group is able to control the reversal
       很有可能不會於可見將來撥回的情況除外。                   of the temporary difference and it is probable that the temporary
       因與有關投資及權益相關的可扣減暫時差額                   difference will not reverse in the foreseeable future. Deferred tax
       而產生的遞延稅項資產僅在可能產生足夠應                   assets arising from deductible temporary differences associated
       課稅溢利以動用暫時差額利益並預期可在可                   with such investments and interests are only recognised to the
       見將來撥回時確認。                             extent that it is probable that there will be sufficient taxable profits
                                             against which to utilise the benefits of the temporary differences
                                             and they are expected to reverse in the foreseeable future.


       遞延稅項資產的賬面值於報告期末作檢討，                   The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the end
       並在不再可能會有足夠應課稅溢利令全部或                   of the reporting period and reduced to the extent that it is no
       部分資產價值被收回時作調減。                        longer probable that sufficient taxable profits will be available to
                                             allow all or part of the asset to be recovered.


       遞延稅項資產及負債乃按預期於負債獲償還                   Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax rates
       或資產變現期間的適用稅率（以報告期末已                   that are expected to apply in the period in which the liability is
       制定或實質制定的稅率（及稅法）為基準）計                  settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rate (and tax laws)
       算。                                    that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end of
                                             the reporting period.

      實際利息法是一種計算金融資產或金融負債                    The effective interest method is a method of calculating the
      的攤銷成本及於有關期間攤分利息收入及利                    amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability and of
      息開支的方法。實際利率是於初步確認時準                    allocating interest income and interest expense over the relevant
      確地透過金融資產或金融負債預計年期或                     period. The effective interest rate is the rate that exactly discounts
      （如適用）於較短期間內將估計日後現金收款                   estimated future cash receipts and payments (including all fees
      及付款（包括屬整體實際利率一部分的所有                    and points paid or received that form an integral part of the
      已付或已收費用及利息、交易成本及其他溢                    effective interest rate, transaction costs and other premiums or
      價或折讓）貼現為賬面淨值的利率。                       discounts) through the expected life of the financial asset or
                                             financial liability, or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the
                                             net carrying amount on initial recognition.

       金融資產在一項或以上違約事件（對該            A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more events
       金融資產估計未來現金流量構成不利影            of default that have a detrimental impact on the estimated
       響）發生時維持信貸減值。金融資產維            future cash flows of that financial asset have occurred.
       持信貸減值的證據包括有關下列事件的            Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired includes
       可觀察數據：                       observable data about the following events:

   •   發行人或借款人的重大財務困難；             •    significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the
                                        borrower;

   •   違反合約，如違約或逾期事件；              •    a breach of contract, such as a default or past due
                                        event;

   •   借款人的貸款人因有關借款人財              •    the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or
       務困難的經濟或合約理由而向借                   contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s
       款人授予貸款人不會另行考慮的                   financial difficulty, having granted to the borrower a
       優惠；                              concession(s) that the lender(s) would not otherwise
                                        consider;

   •   借款人將可能陷入破產或其他財              •    it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter
       務重組；或                            bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation; or

       難而消失。                            asset because of financial difficulties.

       (v)    預期信貸虧損的計量及確認                   (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL
              預期信貸虧損的計量為違約概率、違約                    The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability
              損失率（即違約損失程度）及違約風險的                   of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the
              函數。違約概率及違約損失率乃基於根                    loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default. The
              據前瞻性資料調整的歷史數據評估。預                    assessment of the probability of default and loss given
              期信貸虧損的預估乃無偏概率加權平均                    default is based on historical data adjusted by forward-
              金額，以發生違約的風險為權重確定。                    looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased
                                                   and probability-weighted amount that is determined with
                                                   the respective risks of default occurring as the weights.


              一般而言，預期信貸虧損按本集團根據                    Generally, the ECL is the difference between all contractual
              合約應收的所有合約現金流量與本集團                    cash flows that are due to the Group in accordance with
              預計收取的現金流量的差額估計，並按                    the contract and the cash flows that the Group expects to
              初始確認時釐定的實際利率貼現。                      receive, discounted at the effective interest rate determined
                                                   at initial recognition.

              本集團並無重大集中的信貸風險。銀行                  The Group has no significant concentrations of credit risk.
              結餘及現金、已抵押銀行存款及貿易應                  The carrying amounts of bank balances and cash, pledged
              收款項的賬面金額反映本集團金融資產                  bank deposits and trade receivables represent the Group’s
              的最高信貸風險。於2018年及2017年12             maximum exposure to credit risk in relation to financial
              月31日，所有銀行結餘均存入國有銀行                 assets. As at 31 December 2018 and 2017, all bank balances
              及信譽良好的金融機構，故無重大信貸                  were deposited in reputable financial institutions and were
              風險。管理層預期不會因該等對手方不                  hence without significant credit risk. Management does
              履約而招致任何損失。信貸銷售僅提供                  not expect any losses from non-performance by these
              予信貸記錄良好的選定客戶。本集團已                  counterparties. Credit sales are made to selected customers
              制訂政策確保及時追收未償還的貿易應                  with good credit history. The Group has policies in place to
              收款項。貿易應收款項須應用預期信貸                  ensure that outstanding trade receivables are collected on
              虧損模式。本集團應用國際財務報告準                  a timely basis. Trade receivables are subject to the expected
              則第9號簡化方法計量預期信貸虧損，                  credit loss model. The Group applies IFRS 9 simplified
              於初步確認時，為所有貿易應收款項撥                  approach to measuring expected credit losses which uses a
              備整個存續期內的預期虧損。為計量預                  lifetime expected loss allowance for all trade receivables from
              期信貸虧損，貿易應收款項已根據應佔                  initial recognition. To measure the expected credit losses,
              信貸風險特點及過期天數分類。預期虧                  trade receivables have been grouped based on shared credit
              損率基於銷售的歷史付款情況及出現的                  risk characteristics and the days past due. The expected loss
              相應過往信貸虧損。過往虧損率作出調                  rates are based on the historical payment profiles of sales

              因素的當前及前瞻性資料。                       The historical loss rates are adjusted to reflect current and
                                                 forward-looking information on factors affecting the ability of
                                                 the customers to settle the receivables.

   在管理流動資金風險時，本集團監控並                       In the management of the liquidity risk, the Group monitors
   維持管理層視為足以應付本集團營運所                       and maintains a level of cash and cash equivalents deemed
   需及降低現金流量波動影響的現金及等                       adequate by management to finance the Group’s operations
   同現金項目水平。                                and mitigate the effects of fluctuations in cash flows.


   管理層監控銀行借貸的使用情況。本集                       Management monitors the utilisation of bank borrowings.
   團依賴銀行借貸作為流動資金的主要來                       The Group relies on bank borrowings as a significant source
   源。於2018年12月31日，本集團未動用                   of liquidity. As at 31 December 2018, the Group had available
   的銀行借貸額度為人民幣2,042,750,000                undrawn borrowing facilities of RMB2,042,750,000 (2017:
   元（2017年： 人 民 幣2,991,000,000元 ）。         RMB2,991,000,000). Details of which are set out in note 27.
   詳情載列於附註27。

 本集團業務乃按產品及物業開發的類型劃          The Group’s operations are organised based on the different types
 分。向本公司董事會（即總營運決策人）報告        of products and property development. Information reported to
 以作資源分配及分部表現評估的資料亦按產         the board of directors of the Company, being the chief operating
 品及物業開發的類型呈列。此乃本集團之構         decision maker, for the purposes of resource allocation and
 造基礎。                        assessment of segment performance focuses on types of products
                             and property development. This is the basis upon which the
                             Group is organised.

  均不會導致華夏神舟的賬面總值超過其可收                   of Huaxia Shenzhou and management’s expectations for the
  回總金額。                                 market development. Management believes that any reasonably

34. 退休福利計劃                                   34. Retirement benefits plans
       本集團在中國的僱員乃中國各個地方政府營                        The employees of the Group in the PRC are members of state-
       運的國家退休福利計劃的成員。本集團須向                        managed retirement benefit schemes operated by the respective
       計劃供款指定百分比（按薪酬成本計算），作                       local governments in the PRC. The Group is required to contribute
       為有關福利的資金。本集團的唯一責任是對                        a specified percentage of payroll costs to the schemes to fund the
       該等計劃作出指定供款。                                benefits. The only obligation of the Group with respect to these
                                                  schemes is to make the specified contributions.
